  │ 26 + 3. No complete state preservation (only active texture and bound… │
  │ 27 +                                                                   │
  │ 28 + ### The Fix                                                       │
  │ 29 +                                                                   │
  │ 30 + **File:** `src/main/java/com/seibel/distanthorizons/RenderHelper… │
  │ 31 +                                                                   │
  │ 32 + ```java                                                           │
  │ 33 + public static void drawLods() {                                   │
  │ 34 +     ClientApi.RENDER_STATE.mcModelViewMatrix = getModelViewMatri… │
  │ 35 +     ClientApi.RENDER_STATE.mcProjectionMatrix = getProjectionMat… │
  │ 36 +     ClientApi.RENDER_STATE.clientLevelWrapper = ClientLevelWrapp… │
  │ 37 +                                                                   │
  │ 38 +     // Save state                                                 │
  │ 39 +     boolean alphaTestEnabled = GL11.glIsEnabled(GL32.GL_ALPHA_TE… │
  │ 40 +     boolean blendEnabled = GL11.glIsEnabled(GL32.GL_BLEND);       │
  │ 41 +     FloatBuffer clearColor = BufferUtils.createFloatBuffer(16);   │
  │ 42 +     GL11.glGetFloat(GL11.GL_COLOR_CLEAR_VALUE, clearColor);       │
  │ 43 +                                                                   │

  │ 45 +         GL32.glDisable(GL32.GL_ALPHA_TEST);                       │
  │ 46 +     }                                                             │
  │ 47 +     // Use opaque clear color                                     │
  │ 48 +     GL11.glClearColor(1, 1, 1, 1.0F);                             │

  │ 54 +                                                                   │
  │ 55 +     GL32.glDepthFunc(GL32.GL_LEQUAL);                             │
  │ 56 +                                                                   │
  │ 57 +     // Restore state properly                                     │
  │ 58 +     if (ForgeMain.angelicaCompat == null && alphaTestEnabled) {   │
  │ 59 +         GL32.glEnable(GL32.GL_ALPHA_TEST);                        │
  │ 60 +     }                                                             │
  │ 61 +     if (blendEnabled) {                                           │
  │ 62 +         GL32.glEnable(GL32.GL_BLEND);                             │
  │ 63 +     }                                                             │
  │ 64 +     GL11.glClearColor(clearColor.get(0), clearColor.get(1), clea… │
  │ 65 +                                                                   │
  │ 66 +     GL32.glActiveTexture(oldActiveTex);                           │
  │ 67 +     GL32.glBindTexture(GL32.GL_TEXTURE_2D, oldBoundTex);          │
  │ 68 + }                                                                 │
  │ 69 + ```                                                               │
  │ 70 +                                                                   │
  │ 71 + ### Why This Works                                                │
  │ 72 + - Preserves original OpenGL state instead of assuming defaults    │
  │ 73 + - Prevents transparent clear color from causing black screen      │
  │ 74 + - Alpha test restoration respects original state                  │
  │ 75 +                                                                   │

  │ 21 +         // ... task processing ...                                │
  │ 22 +     }                                                             │
  │ 23 + }                                                                 │
  │ 24 + ```                                                               │
  │ 25 +                                                                   │
  │ 26 + **Issues:**                                                       │
  │ 27 + 1. **15ms time budget** is too aggressive (server tick is 50ms f… │
  │ 28 + 2. **Unbounded chunk load event queue** - iterates all events ev… │
  │ 29 + 3. **Time check only after first task** - at least one task alwa… │
  │ 30 +                                                                   │
  │ 31 + ### The Fix                                                       │
  │ 32 +                                                                   │
  │ 33 + **File:** `src/main/java/com/seibel/distanthorizons/forge/ForgeS… │
  │ 34 +                                                                   │
  │ 35 + ```java                                                           │
  │ 36 + @SubscribeEvent                                                   │
  │ 37 + public void serverTickEvent(TickEvent.ServerTickEvent event) {    │
  │ 38 +     if (event.phase == TickEvent.Phase.END) {                     │
  │ 39 +         // Limit chunk load event processing per tick             │
  │ 40 +         int maxChunkEventsPerTick = 10;                           │
  │ 41 +         int processedChunks = 0;                                  │
  │ 42 +                                                                   │
  │ 43 +         Iterator<ChunkLoadEvent> iterator = chunkLoadEvents.iter… │
  │ 44 +         while (iterator.hasNext() && processedChunks < maxChunkE… │
  │ 45 +             ChunkLoadEvent chunkLoadEvent = iterator.next();      │
  │ 46 +             if (chunkLoadEvent.chunk.isChunkReady()) {            │
  │ 47 +                 this.serverApi.serverChunkLoadEvent(chunkLoadEve… │
  │ 48 +                 iterator.remove();                                │
  │ 49 +                 processedChunks++;                                │
  │ 50 +             } else {                                              │
  │ 51 +                 chunkLoadEvent.age++;                             │
  │ 52 +                 if (chunkLoadEvent.age > 200) {                   │
  │ 53 +                     iterator.remove();                            │
  │ 54 +                 }                                                 │
  │ 55 +             }                                                     │
  │ 56 +         }                                                         │
  │ 57 +                                                                   │

  │ 59 +         long deadline = System.nanoTime() + TimeUnit.MILLISECOND… │
  │ 60 +         boolean processedAtLeastOne = false;                      │
  │ 61 +         while (!taskQueue.isEmpty()) {                            │
  │ 62 +             ScheduledTask<?> scheduledTask = taskQueue.poll();    │
  │ 63 +             if (scheduledTask == null) continue;                  │
  │ 64 +                                                                   │
  │ 65 +             scheduledTask.run();                                  │
  │ 66 +             if (scheduledTask.isLimited() && processedAtLeastOne… │
  │ 67 +                 if (System.nanoTime() >= deadline) break;         │
  │ 68 +             }                                                     │
  │ 69 +             processedAtLeastOne = true;                           │
  │ 70 +         }                                                         │
  │ 71 +     }                                                             │
  │ 72 + }                                                                 │
  │ 73 + ```                                                               │
  │ 74 +                                                                   │
  │ 75 + ### Immediate Workaround                                          │
  │ 76 +                                                                   │

  │ 78 + ```properties                                                     │
  │ 79 + # In DistantHorizons config                                       │
  │ 80 + enableDistantGeneration=false                                     │
  │ 81 + ```                                                               │
  │ 82 +                                                                   │
  │ 83 + ### Why This Works                                                │
  │ 84 + - Limits chunk event processing per tick                          │
  │ 85 + - Conservative 5ms budget leaves headroom for other mods          │
  │ 86 + - Prevents single tick from consuming too much time               │
  │ 87 +                                                                   │

  │  5 + ### Root Cause                                                    │
  │  6 + The `BatchGenerationEnvironment` uses a `LinkedBlockingQueue<Gen… │
  │  7 +                                                                   │
  │  8 + ```java                                                           │

  │ 14 +     GenerationEvent genEvent = GenerationEvent.start(...);        │
  │ 15 +     this.generationEventQueue.add(genEvent);  // No size limit!   │
  │ 16 +     return genEvent.future;                                       │
  │ 17 + }                                                                 │
  │ 18 + ```                                                               │
  │ 19 +                                                                   │
  │ 20 + **Nether-specific issues:**                                       │
  │ 21 + 1. No dimension-specific handling (unlike TerraFirmaCraft detect… │
  │ 22 + 2. Generation may fail silently for Nether chunks, leaving futur… │
  │ 23 + 3. Queue grows without bound if generation can't complete         │
  │ 24 +                                                                   │
  │ 25 + ### The Fix                                                       │
  │ 26 +                                                                   │
  │ 27 + **File:** `src/main/java/com/seibel/distanthorizons/common/wrapp… │
  │ 28 +                                                                   │
  │ 29 + ```java                                                           │
  │ 30 + public class BatchGenerationEnvironment implements IBatchGenerat… │
  │ 31 +     // Change to bounded queue                                    │
  │ 32 +     public final LinkedBlockingQueue<GenerationEvent> generation… │
  │ 33 +         new LinkedBlockingQueue<>(100);  // Max 100 pending even… │
  │ 34 +                                                                   │
  │ 35 +     // Add timeout for generation events                          │
  │ 36 +     private static final long GEN_EVENT_TIMEOUT_MS = 30000;  // … │
  │ 37 +                                                                   │
  │ 38 +     // In updateAllFutures() or similar:                          │

  │ 42 +             GenerationEvent event = iter.next();                  │
  │ 43 +                                                                   │
  │ 44 +             // Check for stuck events                             │
  │ 45 +             long age = System.currentTimeMillis() - event.creati… │
  │ 46 +             if (age > GEN_EVENT_TIMEOUT_MS && !event.future.isDo… │
  │ 47 +                 LOGGER.warn("Generation event timed out after {}… │
  │ 48 +                 event.future.cancel(true);                        │
  │ 49 +                 iter.remove();                                    │
  │ 50 +                 continue;                                         │
  │ 51 +             }                                                     │
  │ 52 +                                                                   │
  │ 53 +             if (event.future.isDone()) {                          │
  │ 54 +                 // ... existing handling ...                      │
  │ 55 +                 iter.remove();                                    │
  │ 56 +             }                                                     │
  │ 57 +         }                                                         │
  │ 58 +     }                                                             │
  │ 59 + }                                                                 │
  │ 60 + ```                                                               │
  │ 61 +                                                                   │
  │ 62 + ### Immediate Workaround                                          │
  │ 63 +                                                                   │

  │ 67 + ```                                                               │
  │ 68 +                                                                   │
  │ 69 + ### Why This Works                                                │
  │ 70 + - Bounded queue prevents memory exhaustion                        │
  │ 71 + - Timeout prevents indefinite hanging                             │
  │ 72 + - Failed events are logged and cleaned up                         │

  │ 14 + 1. The render path is determined before shaders are detected      │

  │ 21 +     return;  // Early return - LODs not rendered!                 │
  │ 22 + }                                                                 │
  │ 23 + ```                                                               │
  │ 24 +                                                                   │
  │ 25 + ### The Fix                                                       │
  │ 26 +                                                                   │
  │ 27 + **File:** `src/main/java/com/seibel/distanthorizons/forge/ForgeC… │

  │ 37 +         DhApiRenderProxy.INSTANCE.setDeferTransparentRendering(t… │
  │ 38 +         LOGGER.info("Shaders detected - enabling deferred render… │
  │ 39 +     }                                                             │
  │ 40 + }                                                                 │
  │ 41 + ```                                                               │
  │ 42 +                                                                   │
  │ 43 + **Alternative in DhApiRenderProxy.java:**                         │
  │ 44 +                                                                   │
  │ 45 + ```java                                                           │
  │ 46 + // Add initialization method                                      │
  │ 47 + public void initializeRenderMode() {                              │
  │ 48 +     IIrisAccessor iris = ModAccessorInjector.INSTANCE.get(IIrisA… │

  │ 51 +     }                                                             │
  │ 52 + }                                                                 │
  │ 53 + ```                                                               │
  │ 54 +                                                                   │
  │ 55 + ### Workaround                                                    │
  │ 56 +                                                                   │
  │ 57 + Disable shaders, load world, then re-enable shaders:              │

  │ 42 + }                                                                 │

  │ 46 +                                                                   │

  │  1 + ## Investigation: Blindness/Underwater Overlay Opacity            │
  │  2 +                                                                   │
  │  3 + Hi @DarkShadow44, I've found the cause of the overlay transparen… │
  │  4 +                                                                   │
  │  5 + ### Root Cause                                                    │
  │  6 + `RenderHelper.drawLods()` unconditionally disables `GL_BLEND`:    │
  │  7 +                                                                   │
  │  8 + ```java                                                           │
  │  9 + // RenderHelper.java:22-37                                        │
  │ 10 + public static void drawLods() {                                   │
  │ 11 +     // ...                                                        │
  │ 12 +     ClientApi.INSTANCE.renderLods();                              │
  │ 13 +     GL32.glDepthFunc(GL32.GL_LEQUAL);                             │
  │ 14 +     if (ForgeMain.angelicaCompat == null) {                       │
  │ 15 +         GL32.glEnable(GL32.GL_ALPHA_TEST);                        │
  │ 16 +     }                                                             │
  │ 17 +     GL32.glDisable(GL32.GL_BLEND);  // Line 37 - breaks overlays! │
  │ 18 +     // ...                                                        │
  │ 19 + }                                                                 │
  │ 20 + ```                                                               │
  │ 21 +                                                                   │
  │ 22 + Minecraft's blindness and underwater overlays rely on `GL_BLEND`… │
  │ 23 +                                                                   │
  │ 24 + ### The Fix                                                       │
  │ 25 +                                                                   │
  │ 26 + **File:** `src/main/java/com/seibel/distanthorizons/RenderHelper… │
  │ 27 +                                                                   │
  │ 28 + ```java                                                           │
  │ 29 + public static void drawLods() {                                   │
  │ 30 +     // ... setup code ...                                         │
  │ 31 +                                                                   │
  │ 32 +     // Save current blend state                                   │
  │ 33 +     boolean blendEnabled = GL11.glIsEnabled(GL32.GL_BLEND);       │
  │ 34 +                                                                   │
  │ 35 +     // DH rendering                                               │
  │ 36 +     ClientApi.INSTANCE.renderLods();                              │
  │ 37 +                                                                   │
  │ 38 +     GL32.glDepthFunc(GL32.GL_LEQUAL);                             │
  │ 39 +                                                                   │
  │ 40 +     // Restore blend state instead of unconditionally disabling   │
  │ 41 +     if (blendEnabled) {                                           │
  │ 42 +         GL32.glEnable(GL32.GL_BLEND);                             │
  │ 43 +     } else {                                                      │
  │ 44 +         GL32.glDisable(GL32.GL_BLEND);                            │
  │ 45 +     }                                                             │
  │ 46 +                                                                   │
  │ 47 +     // ... rest of cleanup ...                                    │
  │ 48 + }                                                                 │
  │ 49 + ```                                                               │
  │ 50 +                                                                   │
  │ 51 + Same fix needed for `drawLodsFade()` method.                      │
  │ 52 +                                                                   │
  │ 53 + ### Why This Works                                                │
  │ 54 + - Preserves Minecraft's blend state for overlays                  │
  │ 55 + - DH's rendering still works correctly                            │
  │ 56 + - Other mods' transparency effects are preserved                  │

  │ 14 +         // Returns to missing set on exception                    │
  │ 15 +         this.queuedGenerationPosSet.remove(missingPos);           │
  │ 16 +         this.missingGenerationPosSet.add(missingPos);             │
  │ 17 +     }                                                             │
  │ 18 + });                                                               │
  │ 19 + ```                                                               │
  │ 20 +                                                                   │
  │ 21 + **Issues:**                                                       │
  │ 22 + 1. No retry limit - may retry infinitely                          │
  │ 23 + 2. No timeout for generation attempts                             │
  │ 24 + 3. Failed positions may never get re-queued                       │
  │ 25 +                                                                   │
  │ 26 + ### The Fix                                                       │
  │ 27 +                                                                   │

  │ 32 + private final ConcurrentHashMap<Long, Integer> retryCountMap = n… │

  │ 38 +     genFuture.whenComplete((result, exception) -> {               │

  │ 42 +                                                                   │
  │ 43 +             if (retries <= MAX_RETRIES) {                         │
  │ 44 +                 LOGGER.warn("Generation failed for {} (retry {}/… │
  │ 45 +                     missingPos, retries, MAX_RETRIES);            │
  │ 46 +                 this.queuedGenerationPosSet.remove(missingPos);   │
  │ 47 +                 this.missingGenerationPosSet.add(missingPos);     │
  │ 48 +             } else {                                              │
  │ 49 +                 LOGGER.error("Generation failed for {} after {} … │
  │ 50 +                     missingPos, MAX_RETRIES);                     │
  │ 51 +                 retryCountMap.remove(missingPos);                 │
  │ 52 +             }                                                     │
  │ 53 +         } else {                                                  │
  │ 54 +             retryCountMap.remove(missingPos);                     │
  │ 55 +         }                                                         │
  │ 56 +     });                                                           │
  │ 57 + }                                                                 │
  │ 58 + ```                                                               │
  │ 59 +                                                                   │

  │ 61 +                                                                   │

  │  2 +                                                                   │
  │  3 + Hi @DarkShadow44, I've analyzed the NPCDBC outline issue.         │
  │  4 +                                                                   │

  │  6 + DH's OpenGL state manipulation in `RenderHelper.drawLods()` like… │

  │ 13 +     }                                                             │
  │ 14 +     GL11.glClearColor(1, 1, 1, 0.0F);  // May clear outline buff… │
  │ 15 +     // ...                                                        │
  │ 16 +     GL32.glDisable(GL32.GL_BLEND);  // Breaks outline transparen… │
  │ 17 + }                                                                 │
  │ 18 + ```                                                               │
  │ 19 +                                                                   │
  │ 20 + ### The Fix                                                       │
  │ 21 +                                                                   │
  │ 22 + Same fix as #56 and #42 - complete OpenGL state preservation:     │
  │ 23 +                                                                   │
  │ 24 + **File:** `src/main/java/com/seibel/distanthorizons/RenderHelper… │
  │ 25 +                                                                   │
  │ 26 + ```java                                                           │
  │ 27 + public static void drawLods() {                                   │
  │ 28 +     // Save ALL relevant GL state                                 │
  │ 29 +     boolean alphaTest = GL11.glIsEnabled(GL32.GL_ALPHA_TEST);     │
  │ 30 +     boolean blend = GL11.glIsEnabled(GL32.GL_BLEND);              │
  │ 31 +     boolean depthTest = GL11.glIsEnabled(GL32.GL_DEPTH_TEST);     │
  │ 32 +     int depthFunc = GL11.glGetInteger(GL32.GL_DEPTH_FUNC);        │
  │ 33 +     FloatBuffer clearColor = BufferUtils.createFloatBuffer(16);   │
  │ 34 +     GL11.glGetFloat(GL11.GL_COLOR_CLEAR_VALUE, clearColor);       │
  │ 35 +                                                                   │
  │ 36 +     // DH rendering                                               │
  │ 37 +     // ...                                                        │
  │ 38 +                                                                   │
  │ 39 +     // Restore ALL state                                          │
  │ 40 +     if (alphaTest) GL32.glEnable(GL32.GL_ALPHA_TEST);             │
  │ 41 +     else GL32.glDisable(GL32.GL_ALPHA_TEST);                      │
  │ 42 +                                                                   │
  │ 43 +     if (blend) GL32.glEnable(GL32.GL_BLEND);                      │
  │ 44 +     else GL32.glDisable(GL32.GL_BLEND);                           │
  │ 45 +                                                                   │
  │ 46 +     if (depthTest) GL32.glEnable(GL32.GL_DEPTH_TEST);             │
  │ 47 +     else GL32.glDisable(GL32.GL_DEPTH_TEST);                      │
  │ 48 +                                                                   │
  │ 49 +     GL32.glDepthFunc(depthFunc);                                  │
  │ 50 +     GL11.glClearColor(clearColor.get(0), clearColor.get(1), clea… │
  │ 51 +     // ...                                                        │
  │ 52 + }                                                                 │
  │ 53 + ```                                                               │
  │ 54 +                                                                   │
  │ 55 + ### Recommendation                                                │
  │ 56 +                                                                   │

  I've completed the corrected forensic analysis for issue #56 based on the

  The Correction
   Aspect       Previous (Wrong)          Corrected
  ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
   Root Cause   RenderHelper.drawLods()   MixinFramebuffer.createDepthTextu
                                          re()
   Location     Gameplay rendering        FML Splash screen initialization
   Phase        During world rendering    During "Initializing game"
   Crash Site   N/A (theorized)           SplashProgress$Texture.<init>() l
                                          ine 621
   Error        N/A                       GL_INVALID_OPERATION: Texture cre
                                          ation: Invalid operation
  Key Findings
  1. Actual Root Cause: MixinFramebuffer makes GL calls (glBindTexture, glTe
     age2D, glTexParameteri) during Framebuffer.createFramebuffer(), which e
     utes during the FML splash screen. This corrupts the GL context while t
     splash thread holds it.
  2. Why Angelica Matters: Angelica replaces FML's SplashProgress entirely,
     assing the problematic code path.
  3. Two Separate Bugs:
    • Splash Screen Crash (init phase) — MixinFramebuffer GL context corrupt
    • Gameplay Black Screen — RenderHelper GL state issues (the original Bat
      finding was correct about this SECOND bug)
  Artifacts Created
  Located in ~/orthogonal-engineering/investigations/distanthorizons_standal
  one/issue_56_corrected/:
  • issue_56_CORRECTED_analysis.json — Complete forensic analysis
  • issue_56_CORRECTED_comment.md — Copy-paste GitHub comment
  • issue_56_secondary_bug.json — Documentation of the RenderHelper gameplay
    ug
  • sha256_manifest.txt — File integrity hashes
  Proposed Fix
  Add splash screen detection to MixinFramebuffer to defer GL calls:
  private boolean isSplashScreenActive() {
      // Check if SplashProgress is still running
      // If yes, delegate to original OpenGlHelper methods
      // If no, use custom GL logic
  }
